2.   Intellectual Property Rights Protection

     The Company attaches great importance to intellectual property protection, and strictly complies
     with national laws and regulations such as the Copyright Law of the People’s Republic of China, the
     Trademark Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Patent Law of the People’s Republic of China, the
     Implementing Regulations of the Trademark Law of the People’s Republic of China, the Implementing
     Regulations of the Copyright Law of the People’s Republic of China , and the Implementing Regulations
     of the Patent Law of the People’s Republic of China. We standardize the application and approval
     procedures for intellectual property rights, copyright management, and procurement processes, and
     continuously advance the construction of the intellectual property management system. While focusing
     on the protection of our own intellectual property rights, we also actively maintain the intellectual
     property rights of others, committed to creating a healthy and sustainable innovation environment.


     The Company’s intellectual property mainly includes categories such as trademarks, domain names,
     copyrights, and patents. In terms of intellectual property protection, we have established a robust
     intellectual property protection mechanism, explicitly stipulating key matters in the application,
     registration, and record processes of intellectual property rights, and continuously track the valid period
     of intellectual property to ensure timely renewal before expiration, thereby effectively safeguarding
     the legitimate rights and interests of the Company. The Company strictly combats intellectual property
     infringement acts, and entrusts professional institutions to continuously monitor the infringement
     risks of film and television projects in online dissemination. Once illegal and non-compliant acts are
     identified, we will take necessary protection measures through legal channels to effectively safeguard
     the legitimate rights and interests of creators and investors. In business cooperation, to avoid infringing
     on the intellectual property rights of others, we effectively regulate the use of third-party intellectual
     property content such as fonts and images through professional retrieval, risk analysis, and signing
     agreements with suppliers, reducing the Company’s infringement risk.


                      Table: Acquisition of Intellectual Property Rights in 2025


                                                              Category of
     Category of Intellectual                                 Intellectual
     Property Rights                Number                    Property Rights          Number

     Trademarks                     81                        Copyrights (Software)    8

     Domain Names                   4                         Copyrights (Works)       2
278 MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025


    Environmental, Social and Governance Report




                To effectively prevent the occurrence of intellectual property infringement events, we implement
                process management for authorized use, and implement controls over purchased copyrights within
                the office network. At the same time, we regularly carry out intellectual property training and promotion
                activities to popularize intellectual property-related legal knowledge among employees and enhance
                their awareness of intellectual property protection. In 2025, we engaged an external professional
                legal team to conduct special compliance training focusing on the IP business. The training covered
                dimensions such as the acquisition of intellectual property rights, compliance points for derivative
                development, dispute resolution, and rights protection strategies, effectively enhancing employees’
                ability to identify and respond to intellectual property infringement events concerning trademarks,
                patents, copyrights, domain names, etc., and improving the Company’s overall intellectual property
                protection effectiveness.


                  Case: IP Compliance Training by External Professional Team

                  In November 2025, targeting the Company’s legal department and IP operation department, we
                  engaged an external law firm to deliver a special training seminar titled “Legal Compliance Guidelines
                  for IP Operation Business of Film and Television Companies”. The training focused on explaining the
                  risk precautions of IP introduction, compliance points during derivative product development, and
                  methods for handling disputes and infringement, providing strong legal support and safeguards for
                  the future business development of the Company’s IP operation department.


          3.    Content Review

                To fulfil its platform responsibilities and foster a clean and healthy online environment, the Company
                strictly complies with relevant laws and regulations, including the Cybersecurity Law of the People’s
                Republic of China and the Administrative Measures for Internet Information Services. A clear content
                review mechanism is stipulated in the Maoyan User Service Agreement, with the aim of providing
                consumers with compliant, healthy, and high-quality information.


                The Company has established a systematic content review system and a content inspection platform to
                conduct comprehensive reviews of user-uploaded text reviews and video content, focusing on identifying
                and filtering information such as extreme and inappropriate remarks, platform advertisements, and
                undesirable promotions. The Company has set up a dual review defense line of “ automated filtering
                + manual assessment”. All content is first screened by digital technology and then reviewed by a
                professional team to ensure the comprehensiveness and accuracy of the review. For films involving
                special themes or high public attention, we implement multiple daily screenings and early monitoring
                and warning measures. During peak release periods – such as the New Year, Spring Festival, Labour
                Day, and summer holiday seasons – screening intensity is dynamically enhanced for selected films.
                During the year, the Company reviewed over 2.68 million pieces of content and handled over 2,700
                pieces of non-compliant content.
                                                               MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025 279


                                       Environmental, Social and Governance Report




     The Company also engages multiple third-party review teams to conduct cross-reviews of content
     across platforms such as the Maoyan APP and the Maoyan mini-program. The review process leverages
     compliance identification, user behavior review, and keyword interception technologies, effectively
     avoiding omissions and misjudgments. At the same time, we have introduced high-response, high-
     stability, and quality-controllable third-party algorithms, adopting a multi-algorithm cross-validation
     method for special scenarios to significantly improve review accuracy. A quality feedback mechanism
     is also in place to timely optimize review strategies and ensure overall content security.


     To establish a robust reporting and response mechanism for content-related issues, the Company has
     set up both a business reporting channel and a customer complaint hotline. The reporting function
     is located in the user comment section of project detail pages. Upon receiving a report, the platform
     system automatically initiates automated review procedures to identify and assess potentially non-
     compliant content. If flagged as non-compliant, the content is immediately hidden. Content that
     passes automated review is forwarded for manual review for secondary verification. Through this
     dual “automated review + manual review” mechanism, the platform ensures that all user reports are
     handled promptly and accurately. Meanwhile, a dedicated customer complaint hotline is available,
     with specialized staff responsible for accepting, handling, and providing feedback on user reporting
     information, ensuring an efficient and smooth reporting process.


     During the year, in response to special rectification requirements and urgent content review tasks, the
     Company organized multiple rounds of emergency practical training. The training was closely aligned
     with real operational scenarios, focusing on areas such as malicious account bans, coordinated fake
     reviews removal, and the review of special short comments. Adopting a “rectification + training +
     practice” approach, the programme strengthened the integration of training with actual work. A total
     of four specialized training sessions were conducted throughout the year, covering approximately 120
     participants. These efforts significantly enhanced reviewers’ response speed, identification accuracy,
     and compliance operation capabilities in complex scenarios, thereby significantly enhancing the risk
     response capabilities of the content review team.


4.   Customer Services

     big data with its service platform, the Company strengthens customer reliance on the platform with

                   Meanwhile, the Company continues to expand the diversification of its service channels. We

                   experience. In 2025, the telephone customer service connection rate reached 97%, with a
                   100% issue resolution rate.


            4.2    Customer Complaint Management

                   We focus on both in-sale and after-sales stages. By combining keyword search with manual
                   quality inspection, we ensure service direction accuracy, enhance service precision, and
                   improve issue resolution rates. The platform actively complies with the regulatory requirements
                   of competent authorities by establishing a precise diversion mechanism for non-local work
                   orders. Particularly for film and performance-related complaints, we implement rapid localized
                   dispatching to achieve “accurate matching and efficient coordination”, effectively improving
                   the response and handling efficiency of customer appeals. Regarding ticketing complaints,
                   we have established a cross-departmental full-process tracking mechanism, strengthening
                   internal coordination and closed-loop feedback until the problem is properly resolved, effectively
                   safeguarding user rights and interests. Customers can complain and report through customer
                   complaint channels such as online customer service and telephone customer service, and the
                   platform assigns dedicated personnel to handle them. Meanwhile, we conduct special reviews
                   of high-frequency and high-risk customer complaint cases, formulate rectification plans, and
                   promote closed-loop issue resolution and process optimization.


                   In 2025, we received a total of 2,67310 customer complaints regarding products or feedback,
                   with a 100.00% customer complaint resolution rate. We also handle customer complaint issues
                   through third-party platforms such as Black Cat Complaints (黑貓投訴) and Xiaofeibao Complaints
                   (消費保投訴), and responded to all cases in a timely and appropriate manner. The response
                   rates for Black Cat Complaints and Xiaofeibao Complaints were both 100%.


            4.3    Customer Satisfaction Survey

                   To enhance after-sales service, we continued to adopt the method of inviting reviews via SMS
                   after the service ends to conduct customer satisfaction surveys, so as to continuously improve
                   customer satisfaction. At the same time, we strictly prohibited customer service staff from guiding
                   customers to leave positive reviews, ensuring that user evaluations are authentic and objective.

                4.4    Service Capability Enhancement

                       (issue resolution rate), etc., and gradually raise the assessment standards based on the time
                       on the job. For management personnel, we set management indicators such as overall team
                       performance completion status, customer service turnover rate, and production task non-
                       compliance rate, to support the continuous development of their professional and management
                       capabilities. At the same time, we regularly compile key data on complaint volume, complaint
                       rate, resolution rate, satisfaction, government complaint volume, and connection rate, and
                       summarize and analyze the statistical results to prepare weekly, monthly, and annual reports.

    IX.   PLATFORM RESPONSIBILITY AND BUSINESS FOUNDATION


          The Company is committed to building a safe, reliable, and efficient service system, always prioritizing user
          rights and interests and information security in its development. The Company establishes long-term, stable,
          responsibility-sharing, and win-win partnerships with suppliers. Furthermore, by establishing a standardized
          business ethics management framework, we continuously strengthen business ethics supervision to create
          a clean and upright business environment.


          1.    Information Security

                Building a trustworthy and controllable responsible platform is the key to ensuring the secure and stable
                operation of the Company’s business. Through strict and systematic information security protection
                mechanism and comprehensive privacy protection measures, the Company safeguards the legitimate
                rights and interests of customers and promotes the continuous upgrade of service experience and trust
                framework. The Company strictly complies with relevant laws and regulations such as the Cyber Security
                Law of the People’s Republic of China and the Data Security Law of the People’s Republic of China,
                establishes a comprehensive information security management system, sets up robust information
                security prevention and control processes, and makes every effort to guarantee network security.


                1.1    Information Security Management System

                       The Company continuously improves the company-level information security system through
                       actively aligning itself with industry regulatory requirements and special action guidelines.
                       In accordance with laws and regulations and business realities, the Company formulates
                       policies such as the Information Security and Confidentiality Regulations and the Data
                       Security Management Regulations applicable to all employees and relevant business units
                       and subsidiaries, clearly defining information security strategies, division of responsibilities,
                       and management processes, and systematically regulating information collection, storage, use,
                       and processing activities, thus providing a solid institutional guarantee for overall information
                       security control.
                                                         MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025 285


                                 Environmental, Social and Governance Report




      The Company continuously improves the information security governance system and
      establishes the Data Security Management Committee to be responsible for the supervision
      and overall coordination of information security work. The Data Security Management Committee
      is supervised by the Board of Directors with members covering the head of the enterprise
      information center and the executives in charge of relevant departments, ensuring that
      information security strategies are implemented in alignment with business development. The
      operations and maintenance department serves as the executive body for information security
      work, specifically implementing the various plans and control requirements of the Data Security
      Management Committee.


1.2   Information Security Audit

      The Company has established and continuously improved a normalized information security
      audit and assessment system. We regularly carry out a combination of internal and external
      security audits every year, systematically reviewing, verifying, and continuously improving the
      Company’s overall information security defense capabilities through institutionalized assessment
      mechanism.


       Internal audit             The Company has established a normalized annual security
                                  audit mechanism covering multiple dimensions such as network
                                  communications, server and terminal security, application and
                                  data security, and security management system, with a view to
                                  comprehensively assessing the compliance and effectiveness of
                                  the security system.

       External audit             The Company always upholds high standards and strict
                                  requirements, promoting the alignment of the information
                                  security management system with industry best practices and
                                  international standards. In 2025, we conducted an information
                                  security protection level assessment, with a result of “Good”. The
                                  Company’s business systems rely on Tencent Cloud infrastructure,
                                  and the cloud platform has comprehensively passed various
                                  security system certifications such as ISO 27001 and ISO 27701.
                                  In addition, the Company has officially initiated Privacy Impact
                                  Assessment (PIA) and ISO 27001 certification work, planning to
                                  complete the assessment and certification by mid-2026, so as to
                                  further enhance the international standardization and compliance
                                  level of security management.

                1.3    Data Leakage Prevention and Emergency System

                       The Company has established and continuously improved a closed-loop management system for
                       data leakage prevention and emergency response covering the entire “prevention – response –
                       handling” process, aiming to reduce data security risks and guarantee business continuity and
                       user rights and interests through anticipatory protection, proactive monitoring, and standardized
                       handling. During the Reporting Period, the Company had no material data leakage incidents.


                        Pre-event               The Company is committed to building active and in-depth security
                        prevention:             defense lines. At the technical level, by integrating security intelligence
                        deploying active        and situational awareness systems, we conduct real-time monitoring
                        defense and access      and active interception of high-risk network addresses, abnormal
                        control                 protocols, and potential malicious software. At the management level,
                                                we strictly follow the data classification and grading system and
                                                implement refined access control. For the acquisition of sensitive
                                                information, we have introduced a strict approval process. If it is
                                                necessary to access sensitive information across departments,
                                                approval must be given by at least two heads or officer-in-charge of
                                                first-level departments. We ensure that data is securely used under
                                                the principle of “minimum necessary”, controlling leakage risks from
                                                the source.


                                                We conduct attack and defense drills every year to actively identify
                                                weaknesses in technology and management by simulating real
                                                attack scenarios. Technical reinforcement, system improvement,
                                                and personnel training is accordingly carried out to continuously
                                                drive the closed-loop improvement and capability iteration of the
                                                security protection system.

                        In-event response:      During data transmission and business operation, the Company
                        implementing            leverages a range of security products and services provided by
                        dynamic monitoring      its cloud platform to conduct continuous security scanning and
                        and risk mitigation     real-time analysis of data flows, ensuring the timely detection of

                                                identified, an assessment will be immediately initiated in accordance
                                                with the Standards for Security Vulnerability Rating and Response
                                                Processing , and corresponding technical measures will be taken
                                                for isolation, blocking, or restoration, preventing the escalation of

1.4   Employee Training on Information Security

      The Company has established a normalized privacy and data security training system covering
      all employees (including part-time and outsourced employees). We set up mandatory modules
      for information security and confidentiality regulations in new employee induction training,
      ensuring that all employees develop basic security awareness upon joining. In daily work,
      the Company regularly pushes information security knowledge, policy interpretations, and
      risk warnings to all employees through internal communication tools. During the Reporting
      Period, we cumulatively completed 11 relevant thematic pushes. In addition, through organizing
      practical training such as attack and defense drills, the Company continuously strengthens
      employees’ practical capabilities in risk prevention and privacy protection under real scenarios.
      To consolidate training effectiveness, the Company requires all employees to pass the annual
      information security assessment, achieving a 100% training coverage rate and assessment
      pass rate, thereby effectively build a solid line of defense for security among all staff.
288 MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025

          2.    Privacy Protection

                The Company attaches great importance to the protection of user privacy rights and interests, and
                strictly complies with laws, regulations, and industry regulatory requirements such as the Personal
                Information Protection Law of the People’s Republic of China , the Measures for Determining the Illegal
                Collection and Use of Personal Information through Apps (《App違法違規收集使用個人信息行為認定
                方法》), and the Regulation of Necessary Personal Information Scope by Common Mobile Internet
                Application (《常見類型移動互聯網應用程序必要個人信息範圍規定》). The Company has established a
                systematic privacy protection management system. We formulated and continuously improved specific
                systems such as the Privacy Policy and the Sensitive Information Definition Standards and Approval
                Process of Sensitive Information Acquisition , covering all products of the Company and explicitly
                defining the full-life-cycle management rules and operating specifications for personal information
                processing. We are committed to comprehensively safeguarding users’ rights to be informed, decide,
                and control their personal information while providing high-quality services.


                2.1    Safeguarding Users’ Control over Personal Information

                       The Company regards safeguarding users’ independent control over their personal information
                       as one of the core measures of privacy protection. We explicitly grant and safeguard users’
                       rights to access, correct, and delete their personal information as well as the right to cancel
                       their accounts in the Maoyan Platform Basic Function Privacy Agreement . Clear and convenient
                       access points and guidance are provided within the App to ensure that users can fully understand
                       and effectively exercise their rights.


                       In practice, we adhere to the “minimum necessary” principle, strictly limiting information
                       collection and use to the minimum scope necessary to achieve product functions and service
                       delivery. Throughout the entire of information processing, the Company upholds the principles
                       of transparency and controllability:


                       •      Access: Before collecting personal information, we proactively and clearly inform users
                              of the purpose, method, scope, and storage period of collection through pop-ups,
                              agreements and other means, and only process it after obtaining users’ valid consent.


                       •      Correct: We provide convenient online functions for registered users, enabling them to
                              access, correct, and update the personal information they submitted at any time.
                                                           MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025 289


                                   Environmental, Social and Governance Report




      •      Delete: When users exercise the right to delete or decide to cancel their accounts on the
             Maoyan app, we will, upon the user’s operation is completed, initiate and complete the
             backend deletion of personal information within 24 hours in strict accordance with internal
             procedures, ensuring that relevant information cannot be recovered, and effectively
             safeguarding users’ privacy rights and interests. When users exercise the right to delete
             or decide to cancel their accounts on the Meituan app or Wallet mini-program, users’
             assets will not be cleared. If users re-register, a new account will be created, which is
             not associated with data from the previous account.


2.2   Supplier and Third-Party Data Management

      To safeguard the security of user data in the cooperative ecosystem, the Company has established
      a data protection management system covering suppliers and business partners. We ensure that
      the entire cooperation process complies with data security and privacy protection requirements
      through a combination of institutional constraints, technical alignment, and qualification reviews.


      In collaborations with suppliers and business partners, we require them to sign the Confidentiality
      Agreement, and explicitly incorporate key terms such as data security responsibilities, scope
      of use, confidentiality obligations, and liabilities for breach into cooperation contracts. During
      the cooperation execution process, by implementing unified security development standards
      and terminal prevention and control requirements, we conduct technical alignment and
      process supervision over key data processing activities. In addition, the Company reviews
      and continuously assesses the relevant security qualifications and protection capabilities of
      partners to ensure they possess a level of information security guarantee commensurate with
      business risks.


      We adhere to the principles of lawfulness, legitimacy, and necessity, undertaking not to rent,
      sell, or illegally provide users’ personal information. Personal information will only be disclosed in
      accordance with legal authorization and subject to strict internal approval procedures, including
      cooperation with lawful requests from competent authorities. We are committed to building
      a secure and trustworthy data processing environment together with partners to effectively
      safeguard user data security and privacy rights and interests.
290 MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025


    Environmental, Social and Governance Report

3.   Supply Chain Management

     The Company insists on integrating sustainability requirements into the full life cycle management
     of suppliers, systematically identifying and controlling relevant risks across various processes from
     development and admission, evaluation and grading, to exit. We take suppliers’ performance in
     environmental protection, labor rights, and business ethics as important considerations, and are
     committed to establishing long-term, stable, responsibility-sharing, and win-win partnerships with
     suppliers.


     3.1   Full-Process Supplier Management

           The Company strictly complies with the requirements of laws and regulations such as the Law of
           the People’s Republic of China on Bid Invitation and Bidding and the Implementation Regulations
           of the People’s Republic of China on Bid Invitation and Bidding . Combined with business
           reality, we formulated a series of internal administrative rules such as the Supplier Information
           Form, Supplier Data Form, Business Ethics Commitment Letter, Supplier Management Rules,
           and Supplier Procurement Practices. In accordance with the Supplier Management Rules,
           we established a full life cycle management system covering the three major processes of
           “admission – evaluation – exit”. Through standardized and process-oriented mechanism, we
           provide a solid resource guarantee for the Company’s business operations.

     4.    Business Ethics

           The Company is committed to creating a clean and upright business environment by building a
           standardized business ethics management framework and continuously strengthening business ethics
           supervision. Meanwhile, we continuously improve the establishment of the business ethics policy
           and system, and conduct training related to business ethics policies to provide efficient guidelines
           for employee compliance management. Regular audits on business ethics-related matters are also
           conducted to ensure that all business activities comply with laws, regulations, and ethical standards,
           assisting the steady development of the enterprise.


           4.1    Business Ethics Management

                  The Company has established a robust business ethics management structure. The Audit
                  Committee under the Board of Directors is fully responsible for the supervision and guidance
                  of the Company’s business ethics management. Business departments, in conjunction with
                  functional departments such as legal affairs, finance, human resources, and administration,
                  build a standardized process system for business ethics, effectively safeguarding the daily
                  management of business ethical behaviors. The internal audit and supervision department
                  audits and supervises the daily management of the Company’s business ethics to ensure that
                  the management mechanism is compliant and effective, and regularly reports the progress of
                  relevant work to the Audit Committee.


                  The Company strictly follows laws and regulations such as the Anti-Unfair Competition Law of
                  the People’s Republic of China and the Interim Provisions on Prohibition of Commercial Bribery.
                  Taking into account its business characteristics, the Company formulated rules and regulations
                  such as the Maoyan Entertainment Anti-Corruption Policy12 to define standards for commercial
                  conduct.

                       We explicitly require all employees to strictly comply with anti-corruption-related content in the
                       Employee Manual. At the same time, we ensure that new employees acknowledge, sign, and
                       comply with the code of business ethics, and we prepare an English version of the Staff Code
                       of Conduct for overseas employees to facilitate their understanding of and compliance with our
                       business ethics provisions. We encourage close attention to and strict self-discipline in their
                       conduct, thereby promoting the Company’s stable and compliant operations.


                       The Company continuously optimizes and improves the anti-corruption management system,
                       resolutely resisting any behaviors that violate business ethics such as corruption, bribery,
                       malpractice, extortion, fraud, and money laundering. It is committed to fostering an upright,
                       open, and transparent working atmosphere, building an operating ecosystem of integrity and
                       honesty, so as to ensure the effective implementation of systems related to business ethics.
                       In 2025, there were no concluded corruption-related litigation cases against the Company or
                       its employees.


                4.2    Business Ethics Audit

                       During the Reporting Period, based on business ethics codes such as the Maoyan Entertainment
                       Anti-Corruption Policy, the Company carried out internal compliance audit work, focusing on
                       reviewing the effectiveness of policy implementation and process compliance, and made
                       improvement recommendations to address identified weaknesses, thereby reinforcing a
                       governance culture of integrity and transparency. The Company formulates the annual audit
                       plan at the beginning of each year, comprehensively assessing the coverage and implementation
                       effectiveness of audit projects in previous years, and defining the audit priorities for the current
                       year, aiming to achieve full coverage of the Company’s core businesses through a three-year
                       audit cycle planning framework. In 2025, the Company’s audit work focused on main business
                       segments such as the performance business unit and the film business unit. Regular audits were
                       conducted on a quarterly basis, with one special audit carried out on average per quarter. The
                       audit scope primarily covered areas involving business ethics such as employee compliance
                       and corruption risks, strictly enforcing red-line requirements such as anti-corruption, anti-fraud,
                       employee conduct management, and unfair competition, thereby continuously strengthening
                       the Company’s ethical governance and internal control effectiveness.
                                                         MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025 297


                                    Environmental, Social and Governance Report




      At the same time, employees are required to conduct a declaration of interests before joining,
      which is submitted to the Company for timely review, to guarantee the effective isolation of
      conflict-of-interest risks.


      Furthermore, the Company conducts comprehensive audits and assessments across four major
      dimensions – external environment risks, business operation risks, internal management risks,
      and financial risks – covering 16 key risk scenarios, thus developing a holistic risk landscape
      map. Leveraging internal control process optimization and the advancing of other related
      initiatives, the Company achieves systematic audit and continuous monitoring of key risks.


4.3   Whistleblower Protection

      The Company maintains a zero-tolerance attitude toward any acts that violate business ethics,
      and strictly complies with internal management policies such as the Maoyan Entertainment
      Whistleblowing Policy. We continuously improve the whistleblowing management mechanism
      by standardizing whistleblowing channels, handling procedures, and whistleblower protection
      measures to safeguard the legitimate rights and interests of whistleblowers.


      To ensure that reported matters are handled in a standardized and efficient manner, we have
      established a clear whistleblowing management process, covering stages such as registration,
      grading, investigation and accountability. The internal audit and supervision department assigns
      dedicated personnel to track and record the entire process. Reported matters are classified and
      managed according to a tiered grading system, with corresponding investigation approaches
      adopted based on their classification. Upon completion of investigations, disciplinary actions
      are imposed based on the severity of violations, and suspected illegal activities are referred to
      relevant authorities in accordance with the law. The internal audit and supervision department
      regularly summarizes report handling, investigation progress, and outcomes, and reports to
      the Audit Committee to form a closed-loop management.
298 MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025


    Environmental, Social and Governance Report




                       Whistleblower protection provisions are clearly stipulated in the Employee Handbook. During the
                       whistleblowing handling process, access to whistleblowing information is strictly restricted, and
                       the identity of whistleblowers must not be disclosed without their written consent, maximizing
                       the protection of whistleblower information security to prevent any form of retaliation such as
                       obstruction, threats, intimidation, and harm.


                       We provide multiple transparent whistleblowing channels, including an email address for
                       reporting clues for fraud on the official website, a whistleblowing hotline, and corporate
                       official account, encouraging real-name or anonymous reporting of various non-compliance
                       or disciplinary violations.


                4.4    Business Ethics Training

                       The Company has established an integrity management system centred on business ethics
                       policies. The code of business ethics and related policies of the Company are incorporated into
                       mandatory onboarding training for new employees. Regular business ethics and compliance
                       training sessions are conducted annually for new hires, existing employees, and directors.
                       Training content covers topics such as the impact of integrity on individuals and the Company,
                       relevant policies and systems and related interpretation, case studies of duty-related crimes,
                       industry anti-corruption dynamics, and business risk scenarios, so as to foster a strong culture
                       of business ethics.
                                                   MAOYAN ENTERTAINMENT / Annual Report 2025 299


                            Environmental, Social and Governance Report




We continuously strengthen integrity awareness among all employees. Through online and offline
training platforms, we regularly push integrity-themed content to strengthen the promotion of
integrity culture to all employees. During the year, we published 3 integrity articles through
the corporate office platform. To assist employees in understanding the Company’s code of
business ethics as soon as possible, training content and case studies are dynamically tailored
to different business lines to help employees accurately identify fraud and bribery risks in
specific business scenarios, thereby enhancing the Company’s overall capabilities in risk
prevention and response related to business ethics. Meanwhile, we disseminate the notice of
the Information Declaration Regarding Non-Conflict of Interest Statement , reminding employees
to proactively report potential conflicts of interest and actively report non-compliant behaviors,
continuously strengthening the integrity awareness of all employees.


In addition, initiatives such as integrity questionnaire surveys and anti-fraud awareness booths
are organized to embed the concepts of honesty and integrity into employees’ daily work,
promoting their deep understanding of business ethics and behavioral norms to strengthen
the foundation of a compliance-driven corporate culture.


In 2025, we conducted two business ethics training sessions for directors, featuring integrity
training sharing, with 9 directors participating. We conducted 4 business ethics training sessions
for new employees, covering 153 newly externally recruited employees and 27 newly recruited
campus graduates. The training content included anti-corruption and malpractice awareness,
declaration of interests, and code of conduct, etc., with an average training duration of 0.5
hours per person and a pass rate of 100% in assessments.
